                const category = { item_category: "US Support Catalog" };
                const lastSlotIdx = MAX_CATEGORY_ITEM - 2;

                for (let i = 2; i <= MAX_CATEGORY_ITEM; i++) {
                    category['item_category' + i] = "";

<script>
    var $header = document.querySelector('.cmp-header') || null;
    var headerMode = localStorage.getItem('header-mode');
    var searchPageUrl = $header ? $header.querySelector('#hiddenSearchPageUrlDiv').getAttribute('searchPageUrl') : "";
    var tabContentId = '#' + headerMode + 'TabContent';
    var tabId = '#' + headerMode + 'Tab';
    // Check if redirect from Search result page
    if(window.location.href.includes(searchPageUrl)) {
        $header.querySelector('.nav-link.active').classList.remove('active');
        $header.querySelector('.tab-pane.active').classList.remove('active');
        document.querySelector(tabId).classList.add('active');
        document.querySelector(tabContentId).classList.add('active');
        if(headerMode === 'pro') {
            $header.querySelector('.nav-tabs-wrapper').classList.add('protab');
            $header.querySelector('.nav-tabs').classList.add('protab');
            $header.querySelector('#consumerTab').classList.add('protab');
            $header.querySelector('#businessTab').classList.add('protab');
        }
    }
</script>

        var parentElm = document.querySelector(".firmware.section .no-component-spacing");
        var softwareDownloadList = parentElm?.querySelector(".software-downloads");
        if (!parentElm || !softwareDownloadList || !messageFirmware) {
            return;
        }
        var messageElm = document.createElement('div');
        messageElm.classList.add('download-message-wrapper');
        messageElm.classList.add('download-message-firmware');
        messageElm.innerHTML = messageFirmware ?? "";
        parentElm.insertBefore(messageElm, softwareDownloadList)
    }

                    'filename': 'imagePROGRAF TX-4200 Firmware Ver.2.03 (Need "Firmware Update Tool" to upgrade/for V1.90 and later)'
                },
                '/content/dam/nw3s/driver-downloads/data/cusa/en/0401093202EN.xml': {
                    'version': 'version 1.90',
                    'linkDownload': 'https://pdisp01.c-wss.com/gdl/WWUFORedirectTarget.do?id=MDQwMDAxMDkzMjAx&cmp=ABR&lang=EN',
                    'filename': 'imagePROGRAF TX-4200 Firmware Ver.1.90 (Need "Firmware Update Tool" to upgrade/for V1.04 and earlierr)'
                }
            }
        }
        const firmwareFiles = {};
        event.detail?.forEach(file => {
            const os = file.os;
            firmwareFiles[os] = DateUtils.compareFileByPostDate(firmwareFiles[os], file);
        });
        let newFirmwareList = [...Object.values(firmwareFiles)];
        let selectorItemTarget = '';
        if (window.productInfo?.productId && messageForProduct[productInfo.productId] && newFirmwareList.length > 0) {
            let messages = messageForProduct[productInfo.productId];
            //show general message
            if (!window.firstLoad) {
                var messageTemplate2 = `<strong>Notice:</strong> <br>Please update this product in the correct order:
                        <ol style="padding: 0;list-style-type: none;">
                            <li>1. First, download and install <a href="#linkDownload190" class="download-message-link"><strong>Firmware Version 1.90</strong></a> from the links below.</li>
                            <li>2. After installation is complete, download and install <strong>Firmware Version 2.01</strong> using this link:<br>
                                <a href="#linkDownload201" class="download-message-link" style="text-decoration: underline;">Firmware Version 2.01 Download</a>
                            </li>
                        </ol>
                        <span>Installing the updates <strong>sequentially</strong> is required.</span>`;
                let arrayKeys = Object.keys(messages);
                var linkDownload190 = "#";
                var linkDownload201 = "#";
                var version190 = '<strong>Firmware Version 1.90</strong>';
                var version201 = '<strong>Firmware Version 2.01</strong>';
                var version201Last = 'Firmware Version 2.01 Download';
                arrayKeys?.forEach(function (fileId) {
                    let fileData = messages[fileId];
                    //message for firmware version 2.01, 2.02, 2.03, 2.04
                    if (
                        fileData['version'] === "version 2.01" ||
                        fileData['version'] === "version 2.02" ||
                        fileData['version'] === "version 2.04" ||
                        fileData['version'] === "version 2.03"
                    ) {
                        linkDownload201 = fileData['linkDownload'];
                    }

                    if (fileData['version'] === "version 2.02") {
                        version201 = '<strong>Firmware Version 2.02</strong>';
                        version201Last = 'Firmware Version 2.02 Download';
                    }

                    if (fileData['version'] === "version 2.03") {
                        version201 = '<strong>Firmware Version 2.03</strong>';
                        version201Last = 'Firmware Version 2.03 Download';
                    }

                    if (fileData['version'] === "version 2.04") {
                        version201 = '<strong>Firmware Version 2.04</strong>';
                        version201Last = 'Firmware Version 2.04 Download';
                    }

                    //link for firmware version 1.90, 1.91, 2.00
                    if (
                        fileData['version'] === "version 1.90" ||
                        fileData['version'] === "version 1.91" ||
                        fileData['version'] === "version 2.00"
                    ) {
                        linkDownload190 = fileData['linkDownload'];
                    }

                    if (fileData['version'] === "version 1.91") {
                        version190 = '<strong>Firmware Version 1.91</strong>';
                    }

                    if (fileData['version'] === "version 2.00") {
                        version190 = '<strong>Firmware Version 2.00</strong>';
                    }
                });
                let messageForItem = messageTemplate2.replace('#linkDownload190', linkDownload190).replaceAll('#linkDownload201', linkDownload201)
                    .replace('<strong>Firmware Version 1.90</strong>', version190).replace('<strong>Firmware Version 2.01</strong>', version201)
                    .replace('Firmware Version 2.01 Download', version201Last)
                showFirmwareMessageInAccordion(messageForItem);
                window.firstLoad = true;
            }
        }
    });
</script>

                <div class="modal instructional-modal how-to-indentify" id="identifyVersionXFModal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
                    <div class="modal-dialog modal-dialog-scrollable" role="document">
                        <div class="modal-content">
                            <div class="modal-header">
                                <h4>How to identify your OS version</h4>
                                <button type="button" class="close-btn" data-dismiss="modal" aria-label="Close">
                                    <span aria-hidden="true">&times;</span>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                            <div class="modal-body scroll-pane">
                                <div class="slider-container">
                                    <div id="main_content-generic_modal-753800172" class="main_content">
                                        <div class="parent">
                                            <div class="modal-instructional-content windows">
                                                <div class="xfpage page basicpage">

    



<div class="xf-content-height">
    


<div class="aem-Grid aem-Grid--12 aem-Grid--default--12 ">
    
    <div class="text aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12"><div class="cmp-text standard-component-padding no-component-spacing black-text">
    <p>To help determine which Windows operating system is running on your computer, please view the below steps: </p>
<p><b>Windows 11</b></p>
<p>Click on the Windows button (located left to the Search at the bottom). </p>
<p>Click on the Settings button to navigate to the system settings. </p>
<p>Scroll to the bottom of the page and click on the About button. </p>
<p>You will be able to find your Windows operating system under the Windows Specifications section. </p>
<p><b>Windows® 10</b> <br />
Click Start or click the Windows button (usually found in the lower-left corner of your screen). <br />
Click Settings. <br />
Click About (which is usually located within the lower left of the screen). The next screen should display the Windows version. </p>
<p><b>Windows 8 or Windows 8.1</b></p>
<p><b>Option1:</b> Swipe in from the upper-right corner of the screen while viewing the desktop in order to open the menu, then select Settings. <br />
Select PC Info. Under Windows edition, the Windows version is shown. </p>
<p><b>Option 2: From the Start Screen</b> <br />
While on the Start screen, type computer. <br />
Right-click on the computer icon. If using touch, press and hold on the computer icon. <br />
Click or tap Properties. Under Windows edition, the Windows version is shown. </p>
<p><b>Windows 7</b> <br />
Click Start or click the Windows button (usually found in the lower-left corner of your screen). <br />
Right-click Computer and select Properties from the menu. The resulting screen should now display the Windows version. </p>

</div>

    

</div>

    
</div>

</div></div>

                                            </div>
                                            <div class="modal-instructional-content linux">
                                                <div class="xfpage page basicpage">

    



<div class="xf-content-height">
    


<div class="aem-Grid aem-Grid--12 aem-Grid--default--12 ">
    
    <div class="rte parbase aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">

    


<div class="rte-cmp standard-component-padding no-component-spacing padding-top- padding-top- padding-bottom- padding-left-right-none"><p><b>Linux</b></p>
<p>To check the version of your Linux operating system (OS), you can use the following commands in your terminal:  </p>
<p>1. uname -r: Displays your Linux kernel version. </p>
<p>2. cat /etc/os-release: Displays your distribution name and version. </p>
<p>3. lsb_release -a: Displays specific details about your Linux distribution and version. </p>
<p>4. You can also use the hostnamectl command to display the Linux kernel version. However, this command is only available on Linux distributions that use systemd by default. </p>
</div>
</div>

    
</div>

</div></div>

                                            </div>
                                            <div class="modal-instructional-content macOS">
                                                <div class="xfpage page basicpage">

    



<div class="xf-content-height">
    


<div class="aem-Grid aem-Grid--12 aem-Grid--default--12 ">
    
    <div class="rte parbase aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12">

    


<div class="rte-cmp standard-component-padding no-component-spacing padding-top- padding-top- padding-bottom- padding-left-right-none"><p>To help determine which Mac operating system is running on your computer, select the Apple menu in the upper-left corner of your screen and choose About This Mac.</p>
<p> </p>
<p>Upon selecting, you should see the macOS name followed by the version number.</p>
</div>

<div class="rte-cmp standard-component-padding no-component-spacing padding-top- padding-top- padding-bottom- padding-left-right-none"><h2 style="	text-align: center;
">CANON PROFESSIONAL DIGITAL VIDEO EQUIPMENT LIMITED WARRANTY FOR PRODUCTS<br />
PURCHASED IN THE UNITED STATES<br />
 </h2>
<p>The limited warranty set forth below is given by Canon U.S.A., Inc. (&#39;Canon USA&#39;) with respect to Canon Professional Digital Video Equipment (the &#39;Equipment&#39;) packaged with this limited warranty and purchased in the United States. This limited warranty is only effective upon presentation of your Bill of Sale or other proof of purchase. The Equipment is warranted to the original end-user purchaser, when delivered in new condition in its original container, under normal use against defective materials or workmanship as follows: Parts: Defective parts will be exchanged for new parts or comparable rebuilt parts for a period of ONE YEAR from the date of original purchase. Labor: For a period of ONE YEAR from the date of original purchase, labor will be provided free of charge by Canon USA&#39;s factory service centers or designated service facilities located in the United States. When returning Equipment under this warranty, you must pre-pay the shipping charges, and you must enclose a copy of the Bill of Sale or other proof of purchase with a complete explanation of the problem. During the ONE-YEAR warranty period, repairs will be made and the Equipment will be return-shipped to you free of charge. For repairs after the warranty period is over, you will be given an estimate of the cost of repair and an opportunity to approve or disapprove of the repair expense before it is incurred. If you approve, repairs will be made and the Equipment will be returned to you at your risk and expense. If you disapprove, we will return-ship the Equipment to you at no charge to you to an address within the United States.</p>
<p> </p>
<p><b style="	font-size: 1.125em;
	background-color: transparent;
">This limited warranty only applies if the Equipment is used in conjunction with compatible computer equipment and compatible software, as to which items Canon USA will have no responsibility. Canon USA shall have no responsibility under this limited warranty for use of the Equipment in conjunction with incompatible peripheral equipment and/or incompatible software. Non-Canon brand peripheral equipment and software which may be distributed with, or factory loaded on, the Equipment, are sold &#39;AS IS&#39; without warranty of any kind by Canon USA, including any implied warranty regarding merchantability or fitness for a particular purpose. The sole warranty with respect to such non-Canon brand items is given by the manufacturer or producer thereof. If the Equipment contains a hard disk drive, Canon USA recommends that data stored on that drive be duplicated or backed up to prevent its loss in the event of failure or other malfunction of such drive.</b></p>
<p> </p>
<p> </p>
<p>In order to obtain warranty service, please contact the authorized Canon retail dealer from whom you purchased the Equipment or contact the CANON INFORMATION CENTER AT <b>855-CINE-EOS</b> (855-246-3367) or on the internet at <b>pro.usa.canon.com/support.</b> You will be directed to the nearest service facility for your Equipment.</p>
<p>This Limited Warranty covers all defects encountered in normal use of the Equipment and does not apply in the following cases:</p>
<ol start="1" style="	list-style-type: upper-alpha;
"><li>Loss or damage to the Equipment due to abuse, mishandling, accident improper maintenance, use of non-Canon accessories or failure to follow operating, maintenance or environmental instructions prescribed in Canon USA&#39;s user&#39;s manual;</li><li>If the Equipment is defective as a result of leaking batteries, sand, dirt or water damage;</li><li>If defects or damages are caused by the use of parts or supplies (other than those sold by Canon USA) that cause damage to the Equipment or that cause abnormally frequent service calls or service problems;</li><li>If defects or damages are caused by service other than Canon USA&#39;s factory service centers or authorized service facilities;</li><li>Any internal modification to product hardware or firmware;</li><li>Any applicable fee for Maintenance of the Equipment;</li><li>If the Equipment has had its serial number or dating altered or removed.</li></ol>
<p>This Limited Warranty does not apply to Equipment purchased outside the United States. This Limited Warranty does not apply to accessories or consumables for the Equipment, which are sold &#34;AS IS&#34;, without warranty of any kind by Canon USA. Please retain this warranty card and your Bill of Sale as a permanent record of your purchase. This card ensures that you are contacted promptly should there be a safety inspection, modification or product recall under applicable laws or regulations.</p>
<p><b>NO IMPLIED WARRANTY, INCLUDING MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE APPLIES TO THIS EQUIPMENT AFTER THE APPLICABLE PERIOD OF EXPRESS WARRANTY OR GUARANTY (EXCEPT AS MENTIONED ABOVE) GIVEN BY ANY PERSON, FIRM OR CORPORATION WITH RESPECT TO THIS EQUIPMENT SHALL BIND THE UNDERSIGNED (SOME STATES DO NOT ALLOW LIMITATIONS ON HOW LONG AN IMPLIED WARRANTY LASTS, SO THE ABOVE LIMITATION OR EXCLUSION MAY NOT APPLY TO YOU). CANON USA SHALL NOT BE LIABLE FOR LOSS OF REVENUES OR PROFITS, EXPENSE FOR SUBSTITUTE EQUIPMENT OR SERVICE, STORAGE CHARGES, LOSS OR CORRUPTION OF DATA, INCLUDING WITHOUT LIMITATION, LOSS OR CORRUPTION OF DATA STORED ON THE EQUIPMENT&#39;S HARD DRIVE, OR ANY OTHER SPECIAL, INCIDENTAL OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES CAUSED BY THE USE, MISUSE OR INABILITY TO USE THE EQUIPMENT, REGARDLESS OF THE LEGAL THEORY ON WHICH THE CLAIM IS BASED, AND EVEN IF CANON USA HAS BEEN ADVISED OF THE POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGES. NOR SHALL RECOVERY OF ANY KIND AGAINST CANON USA BE GREATER THAN THE PURCHASE PRICE OF THE EQUIPMENT SOLD BY CANON USA AND CAUSING THE ALLEGED DAMAGE. WITHOUT LIMITING THE FOREGOING, YOU ASSUME ALL RISK AND LIABILITY FOR LOSS, DAMAGE OR INJURY TO YOU AND YOUR PROPERTY AND TO OTHERS AND THEIR PROPERTY ARISING OUT OF USE, MISUSE OR INABILITY TO USE THE EQUIPMENT NOT CAUSED DIRECTLY BY THE NEGLIGENCE OF CANON USA (SOME STATES DO NOT ALLOW THE EXCLUSION OR LIMITATION OF INCIDENTAL OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES, SO THE ABOVE LIMITATION MAY NOT APPLY TO YOU). THIS WARRANTY SHALL NOT EXTEND TO ANYONE OTHER THAN THE ORIGINAL PURCHASER OF THIS EQUIPMENT OR THE PERSON FOR WHOM IT WAS PURCHASED AS A GIFT.</b></p>

            <div class="allegiance-script" style="display: none;"> 



var surveyURL = &#39;https://canonusa.esaas.inmoment.com/cgi-bin/qwebcorporate.dll?idx=M6BSBQ&#39;;

var mcxSiteInterceptParameters = {

surveyURL: surveyURL, //string

showOnLoad: true, //boolean

invitationID: undefined, //string

probability: 33, //number (0 - 100 (%))

            <div class="allegiance-script" style="display: none;"> 



(function(w){

&#34;use strict&#34;;

//Check to make sure custom parameters exist

if(typeof w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters === &#39;undefined&#39;) {

//Backward compatibility (for customers who have &lt;v1.3)

if(typeof w.allegianceSiteInterceptParameters !== &#39;undefined&#39;) {

w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters = w.allegianceSiteInterceptParameters;

console.warn(&#34;The parameters variable (allegianceSiteInterceptParameters) needs to be renamed to mcxSiteInterceptParameters inside of the client extensibility. allegianceSiteInterceptParameters may be deprecated in future versions.&#34;);

} else {

console.warn(&#34;mcxSiteInterceptParameters undefined, ensure that it is defined in client extensibility and that the site intercept script is loaded in after the client extensibility script. Default parameters used.&#34;);

w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters = &#34;&#34;;

}

}



var isArray = function() { return w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters &amp;&amp; w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters.constructor === Array &amp;&amp; w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters.length &gt; 0; };

if(isArray()) {

var MCX_DEFAULT = &#39;MCX_DEFAULT&#39;;

var mcxUniqueID = w.mcxUniqueID || MCX_DEFAULT;

//Check over each item to see if a uniqueID matches

for(var i = 0; i &lt; w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters.length; i++) {

if(w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters[i].uniqueID &amp;&amp; mcxUniqueID == w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters[i].uniqueID) {

w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters = w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters[i];

}

}

//If no matches were found, attempt to assign default

if(isArray()) {

for(var j = 0; j &lt; w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters.length; j++) {

if(w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters[j].uniqueID &amp;&amp; MCX_DEFAULT == w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters[j].uniqueID) {

w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters = w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters[j];

}

}

//If no default was assigned, use the first item

if(isArray()) {

w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters = w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters[0];

}

}

}



var mcxPresetParam = w.mcxSiteInterceptParameters;



w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit = {

//Parameters used for customizing script

parameters: {

//Defaults

surveyURL: typeof mcxPresetParam.surveyURL === &#39;string&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.surveyURL : &#34;&#34;,

showOnLoad: typeof mcxPresetParam.showOnLoad === &#39;boolean&#39; ? false : false,

invitationID: typeof mcxPresetParam.invitationID === &#39;string&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.invitationID : &#34;&#34;,

probability: typeof mcxPresetParam.probability === &#39;number&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.probability : 100, //0 - 100%

width: typeof mcxPresetParam.width === &#39;number&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.width : 550, //px

height: typeof mcxPresetParam.height === &#39;number&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.height : 520, //px

cookieID: typeof mcxPresetParam.cookieID === &#39;string&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.cookieID : &#34;&#34;,

expireDaysIfYes: typeof mcxPresetParam.expireDaysIfYes === &#39;number&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.expireDaysIfYes : 30, //days

expireDaysIfNo: typeof mcxPresetParam.expireDaysIfNo === &#39;number&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.expireDaysIfNo : 30, //days

delay: typeof mcxPresetParam.delay === &#39;number&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.delay : 0, //ms

waitUntilClose: typeof mcxPresetParam.waitUntilClose === &#39;boolean&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.waitUntilClose : false,

placeholderURL: typeof mcxPresetParam.placeholderURL === &#39;string&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.placeholderURL : &#34;&#34;,

showInline: typeof mcxPresetParam.showInline === &#39;boolean&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.showInline : false,

inlineID: typeof mcxPresetParam.inlineID === &#39;string&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.inlineID : &#34;mcxSurveyDialog&#34;,

inlineWrapperID: typeof mcxPresetParam.inlineWrapperID === &#39;string&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.inlineWrapperID : &#34;mcxSurveyWrapper&#34;,

inlineCloseID: typeof mcxPresetParam.inlineCloseID === &#39;string&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.inlineCloseID : &#34;mcxSurveyClose&#34;,

cleanseCookie: typeof mcxPresetParam.cleanseCookie === &#39;boolean&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.cleanseCookie : false,

debug: typeof mcxPresetParam.debug === &#39;boolean&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.debug : false,

ignoreWarning: typeof mcxPresetParam.ignoreWarning === &#39;boolean&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.ignoreWarning : false,

enabled: typeof mcxPresetParam.enabled === &#39;boolean&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.enabled : true,

pageVisit: typeof mcxPresetParam.pageVisit === &#39;number&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.pageVisit : 4,

mcxBlackList: typeof mcxPresetParam.mcxBlackList === &#39;object&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.mcxBlackList : [],

domainPath: typeof mcxPresetParam.domainPath === &#39;string&#39; ? mcxPresetParam.domainPath : &#34;&#34;

},



takeSurvey: false, //Script-level variable, leave false.

surveyWindow: undefined, //Survey window, leave undefined.

surveyURLParams: undefined,

cachedHTML: undefined,



//Called when the page is loaded. Executes logic to determine whether to show the invitation or not.

onPageLoad: function () {

w.mcxLog(&#39;onPageLoad&#39;);

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.cookieVisitCount();

if(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.uniqueID) { w.mcxLog(&#39;uniqueID: &#39; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.uniqueID); }

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyURLParams = sessionStorage[&#34;mcx.surveyURLParams&#34;];

if (!w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyURLParams) { sessionStorage[&#34;mcx.surveyURLParams&#34;] = &#39;[{}]&#39;; }

var rand = Math.floor(Math.random() * 100);

if(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.probability &lt; 1 &amp;&amp; w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.probability &gt; 0) {

var decimal = w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.probability;

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.probability = Math.floor(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.probability * 100);

w.mcxWarn(&#39;mcxSiteIntercept warning: The script detected a probability between 0 and 1. Probability should be a whole number between 0 and 100. The script has converted it to a whole number: &#39; + decimal + &#39; -&gt; &#39; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.probability);

}

if(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.probability &lt;= 0) {

w.mcxWarn(&#39;mcxSiteIntercept warning: Probability it set to &#39; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.probability + &#39;. It must be greater than 0 to have any chance of displaying the survey.&#39;);

}





//Check if random number is less than probability (less than means probability met) and that survey is enabled

if (rand &lt; w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.probability &amp;&amp; w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.enabled &amp;&amp; parseInt(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.RgetCookie(&#39;McxPageVisit&#39;)) &gt;= w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.pageVisit &amp;&amp; w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.McxBlackList() === false){

//If no cookie exists, and showOnLoad is true, display the survey modal

if (!w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.hasCookie() &amp;&amp; w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.showOnLoad) {

if(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.invitationID.length &gt; 0) {

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.showModal();

} else {

w.mcxLog(&#39;delay &#39; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.delay + &#39; ...&#39;);

w.setTimeout(function () {

w.mcxLog(&#39;... delay &#39;);

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.popUp();

}, w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.delay);

}

}

} else {

if(!w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.enabled) {

w.mcxLog(&#39;Survey is disabled&#39;);

} else {

w.mcxLog(&#39;Probability fail. Chance to succeed: &#39; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.probability + &#39;%&#39;);

}

}

},

//Page visit count

cookieVisitCount: function(){

if(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.RgetCookie(&#39;McxPageVisit&#39;) == &#34;&#34; ){

document.cookie=&#34;McxPageVisit= 1; path=/; domain=&#34;+(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.domainPath)+&#34;;&#34;;



} else {

var pageVisit = parseInt(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.RgetCookie(&#39;McxPageVisit&#39;));

document.cookie=&#34;McxPageVisit=&#34; +(pageVisit + 1)+ &#34;; path=/; domain=&#34;+(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.domainPath)+&#34;;&#34;;

}



},

RgetCookie: function(cname) {

var name = cname + &#34;=&#34;;

var ca = document.cookie.split(&#39;;&#39;);

for(var i=0; i&lt;ca.length; i++) {

var c = ca[i];

while (c.charAt(0)==&#39; &#39;) c = c.substring(1);

if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) return c.substring(name.length,c.length);

}

return &#34;&#34;;

},

McxBlackList: function(){

var checkArray = w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.mcxBlackList;

var aL = checkArray.length;

var curUrl = window.location.href;

for (var i = 0; i &lt; aL; i++){

var testEx = RegExp(checkArray[i]);

var finalCheck = testEx.test(curUrl);

if (finalCheck){ return true; }

}

return false;

},

//Function to add a parameter to URL

addUrlParameter: function (value, name) {

w.mcxLog(&#39;Added URL parameter: [&#39; + value + &#39;, &#39; + name + &#39;]&#39;);

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyURLParams = sessionStorage[&#34;mcx.surveyURLParams&#34;];

var object = JSON.parse(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyURLParams);

object[0][value] = name;

sessionStorage[&#34;mcx.surveyURLParams&#34;] = JSON.stringify(object);

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyURLParams = sessionStorage[&#34;mcx.surveyURLParams&#34;];

},



//Function to remove a parameter from URL

removeUrlParameter: function (value) {

w.mcxLog(&#39;Removed URL parameter: [&#39; + value + &#39;]&#39;);

var object = JSON.parse(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyURLParams);

delete object[0][value];

sessionStorage[&#34;mcx.surveyURLParams&#34;] = JSON.stringify(object);

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyURLParams = sessionStorage[&#34;mcx.surveyURLParams&#34;];

},



//Converts the URL parameters to a query string that will get appended to the survey URL

urlParametersToQueryString: function() {

w.mcxLog(&#39;urlParametersToQueryString ...&#39;);

if(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyURLParams) {

var parameters = JSON.parse(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyURLParams)[0];

var queryString = [];

for(var property in parameters) {

if(parameters.hasOwnProperty(property)) {

queryString.push(property + &#34;=&#34; + parameters[property]);

}

}

w.mcxLog(&#39;... urlParametersToQueryString &#39;);

return &#34;&amp;&#34; + queryString.join(&#39;&amp;&#39;);

} else { return &#34;&#34;; }

},



//Creates a cookie with the given parameters

createCookie: function (name, value, days) {

w.mcxLog(&#39;createCookie: [&#39; + name + &#39;, &#39; + value + &#39;, &#39; + days + &#39;]&#39;);

var date = new Date();

date.setTime(date.getTime() + (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));

w.docCookies.setItem(name, value, date);

},



hasCookie: function() {

var hasCookie = w.docCookies.hasItem(&#39;mcxSurveyQuarantine&#39; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.cookieID, w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.cleanseCookie);

w.mcxLog(&#39;hasCookie: &#39; + &#39;mcxSurveyQuarantine&#39; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.cookieID + &#39;: &#39; + hasCookie);

return hasCookie;

},



//Called when page is closed or domain is changed.

onPageClose: function () {

w.mcxLog(&#39;onPageClose&#39;);

if(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.enabled) {

//w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.addUrlParameter(&#34;mcxReferrerURL&#34;, encodeURIComponent(w.location.href));

if (w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.takeSurvey &amp;&amp; w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyWindow &amp;&amp; w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyWindow.opener &amp;&amp; !w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyWindow.opener.closed) {

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyWindow.location = (w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.surveyURL + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.urlParametersToQueryString());

} else { return; }

}

},



//Brings up the survey

popUp: function () {

w.mcxLog(&#39;popUp&#39;);

var rand = Math.floor(Math.random() * 100);

if (!w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.hasCookie() &amp;&amp; w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.enabled &amp;&amp; rand &lt; w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.probability) {

var strWindowFeatures = &#34;menubar=0,location=yes,resizable=yes,scrollbars=yes,toolbar=0,status=yes,modal=yes,width=&#34; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.width + &#34;,height=&#34; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.height;

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.takeSurvey = true;

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.createCookie(

(&#39;mcxSurveyQuarantine&#39; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.cookieID),

(&#39;mcxSurveyQuarantine&#39; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.cookieID),



w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.expireDaysIfYes);

var inv = document.getElementById(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.invitationID);

if(inv) { inv.style.display = &#39;none&#39;; }

if(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.waitUntilClose) {

//Opens window and shows placeholderURL

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyWindow = w.open(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.placeholderURL, &#34;_blank&#34;, strWindowFeatures);

//w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.surveyWindow.blur();

//window.global.focus();

} else {

//Logic for showing survey on the page rather than a separate window

if(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.showInline) {

//Make sure elem exists on page, otherwise create a DIV with inlineID

if(!elem) {

elem = document.createElement(&#39;div&#39;);

elem.setAttribute(&#34;id&#34;, w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.inlineID);

document.body.appendChild(elem);

}

//Preserve existing HTML

if(typeof w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.cachedHTML === &#39;undefined&#39;) { w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.cachedHTML = elem.innerHTML; }

//Inject additional required HTML and survey

&#39;&lt;div id=&#34;&#39; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.inlineWrapperID + &#39;&#34;&gt;&#39; +

&#39;&lt;div id=&#34;&#39; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.inlineCloseID + &#39;&#34;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&#39; +

&#39;&#34; width=&#34;&#39; + w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.width + &#39;&#34; height=&#34;&#39; +

elem.style.display = &#39;block&#39;;



//Event listener for closing the dialog

var el = document.getElementById(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.inlineCloseID);

if (el.addEventListener) { el.addEventListener(&#34;click&#34;, w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.closeSurveyModal, false); }

else { el.attachEvent(&#34;onclick&#34;, w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.closeSurveyModal); }

} else {

}

}

},



//Hides modal

//Accepts the invitation.

acceptSurvey:function() {

//Creates a quarantine cookie and hides the invitation.

//Shows the invitation

},



addOnLoadEvent: function (func) {

var oldonload = w.onload;

if (typeof w.onload != &#39;function&#39;) {

w.onload = func;

} else {

w.onload = function () {

if (oldonload) { oldonload(); }

func();

};

}

},

addBeforeUnLoadEvent: function (func) {

var oldonload = w.onbeforeunload;

if (typeof w.onbeforeunload != &#39;function&#39;) {

w.onbeforeunload = func;

} else {

w.onbeforeunload = function () {

if (oldonload) { oldonload(); }

func();

};

}

},

var oldonload = w.onunload;

if (typeof w.onunload != &#39;function&#39;) {

w.onunload = func;

} else {

w.onunload = function () {

if (oldonload) { oldonload(); }

func();

};

}

}

};



w.docCookies = {

getBrowser: function () {

// Opera 8.0+

var isOpera = (!!window.opr &amp;&amp; !!opr.addons) || !!window.opera || navigator.userAgent.indexOf(&#39; OPR/&#39;) &gt;= 0;

// Firefox 1.0+

var isFirefox = typeof InstallTrigger !== &#39;undefined&#39;;

// Safari 3.0+ &#34;[object HTMLElementConstructor]&#34;

var isSafari = /constructor/i.test(window.HTMLElement) || (function (p) { return p.toString() === &#34;[object SafariRemoteNotification]&#34;; })(!window[&#39;safari&#39;] || (typeof safari !== &#39;undefined&#39; &amp;&amp; safari.pushNotification));

// Internet Explorer 6-11

var isIE = /*@cc_on!@*/false || !!document.documentMode;

// Edge 20+

var isEdge = !isIE &amp;&amp; !!window.StyleMedia;

// Chrome 1+

var isChrome = !!window.chrome &amp;&amp; !!window.chrome.webstore;

return {

isOpera: isOpera,

isFirefox: isFirefox,

isSafari: isSafari,

isIE: isIE,

isEdge: isEdge,

isChrome: isChrome

}

},

getItem: function (sKey) {

if (!sKey) { return null; }

return decodeURIComponent(document.cookie.replace(new RegExp(&#34;(?:(?:^|.*;)\\s*&#34; + encodeURIComponent(sKey).replace(/[\-\.\+\*]/g, &#34;\\$&amp;&#34;) + &#34;\\s*\\=\\s*([^;]*).*$)|^.*$&#34;), &#34;$1&#34;)) || null;

},

setItem: function (sKey, sValue, vEnd, sPath, sDomain, bSecure) {

if (!sKey || /^(?:expires|max\-age|path|domain|secure)$/i.test(sKey)) { return false; }

var sExpires = &#34;&#34;;

if (vEnd) {

switch (vEnd.constructor) {

case Number:

sExpires = vEnd === Infinity ? &#34;; expires=Fri, 31 Dec 9999 23:59:59 GMT&#34; : &#34;; max-age=&#34; + vEnd;

break;

case String:

sExpires = &#34;; expires=&#34; + vEnd;

break;

case Date:

sExpires = &#34;; expires=&#34; + vEnd.toUTCString();

break;

}

}

var browser = this.getBrowser();

if (browser.isIE || browser.isEdge || !McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.domainPath) {

w.mcxLog(&#39;Building cookie without domain. IE: &#39; + browser.isIE + &#39;; Edge: &#39; + browser.isEdge + &#39;; domainPath: not used(IE)&#39;, true);

document.cookie = encodeURIComponent(sKey) + &#34;=&#34; + encodeURIComponent(sValue) + sExpires + &#34;; path=/&#34;;

} else {

w.mcxLog(&#39;Building cookie with domain. domainPath: &#39; + McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.domainPath, true);

document.cookie = encodeURIComponent(sKey) + &#34;=&#34; + encodeURIComponent(sValue) + sExpires + &#34;; domain=&#34; + McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.domainPath + &#34;; path=/&#34;;

}

return true;

},

removeItem: function (sKey) {

if (!this.hasItem(sKey)) { return false; }

document.cookie = encodeURIComponent(sKey) + &#34;=; expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 GMT; domain=&#34; + McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.domainPath + &#34;; path=/&#34;;

return true;

},

hasItem: function (sKey, cleanseCookie) {

if (!sKey) { return false; }

if (cleanseCookie === true) {

this.removeItem(sKey);

}

return (new RegExp(&#34;(?:^|;\\s*)&#34; + encodeURIComponent(sKey).replace(/[\-\.\+\*]/g, &#34;\\$&amp;&#34;) + &#34;\\s*\\=&#34;)).test(document.cookie);

},

keys: function () {

var aKeys = document.cookie.replace(/((?:^|\s*;)[^\=]+)(?=;|$)|^\s*|\s*(?:\=[^;]*)?(?:\1|$)/g, &#34;&#34;).split(/\s*(?:\=[^;]*)?;\s*/);

for (var nLen = aKeys.length, nIdx = 0; nIdx &lt; nLen; nIdx++) { aKeys[nIdx] = decodeURIComponent(aKeys[nIdx]); }

return aKeys;

}

};



w.mcxWarn = function(warning, ignorePrefix) {

var consoleWarn = typeof console !== &#34;undefined&#34; &amp;&amp; console.warn &amp;&amp; w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.ignoreWarning === false;

if(consoleWarn) {

var prefix = ignorePrefix ? &#39;&#39; : &#39;mcxSiteIntercept: &#39;;

console.warn(prefix + warning);

}

};

w.mcxLog = function(log, ignorePrefix) {

var consoleLog = typeof console !== &#34;undefined&#34; &amp;&amp; console.log &amp;&amp; w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.parameters.debug === true;

if(consoleLog) {

var prefix = ignorePrefix ? &#39;&#39; : &#39;mcxSiteIntercept: &#39;;

console.log(prefix + log);

}

};

w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.addOnLoadEvent(w.McxSiteInterceptOnExit.onPageLoad); //ensures that other events are retained

w.mcxLog(&#34;script loaded&#34;);

}(this));

<script>
   var checkBrowser = checkIeBrowser();

   if(checkBrowser == true) {
      var isVisitSystem = localStorage.getItem("isVisit"),
              pathName = window.location.pathname.replace("/content/canon/", ""),
              isHomePage = !pathName || pathName === "/" || (/^\w+[\-\w]+.html$/).test(pathName),
              isAuthorPage = document.getElementById("canon-not-apply-style-opionlab") && document.getElementById("canon-not-apply-style-opionlab").value === "true",
              bestBrowser = document.getElementById('best_browser_experience');

      localStorage.setItem('isVisit', true);

      if(!isVisitSystem && isHomePage && !isAuthorPage) {
         document.getElementById('best_browser_experience').style.display = "block";

         document.getElementById('best_browser_close_modal').onclick = function() {
            document.getElementById('best_browser_experience').style.display = "none";
         }
      }
   }

   function checkIeBrowser() {
      var isIE = false;
      var ua = window.navigator.userAgent;
      var old_ie = ua.indexOf('MSIE ');
      var new_ie = ua.indexOf('Trident/');

      if ((old_ie > -1) || (new_ie > -1)) {
         isIE = true;
      }

      if ( isIE ) {
         return true;
      }else {
         return false;
      }
   }
</script></div>

//update aria-level in footer
window.addEventListener("load", function () {
    var footerContainer = document.querySelector("#footer-container");
    if (footerContainer) {
        footerContainer.querySelectorAll("[aria-level='1']").forEach(function(item, index){item.setAttribute("aria-level", "2")});
    }
});
</script>

<!-- Start Adding Accessibility Statement skip link-->
<style>
    .accessibility-statement-link {
        position: fixed;
        left: -9999px;
        z-index: 99999999999;
        background-color: #eee;
        color: #333;
        padding: 10px;
        text-decoration: none;
    }

    .accessibility-statement-link:focus-visible,
    .accessibility-statement-link:focus {
        left: 0;

<script>
    var forceFocus = false;
    function handleEventFocusOfDialog(e) {
        if (forceFocus) {
            let firstElm = document.querySelector("#onetrust-banner-sdk");
            let lastElm = document.querySelector("#onetrust-banner-sdk #onetrust-accept-btn-handler");
            if (e.shiftKey && e.keyCode == 9 && e.target.isEqualNode(firstElm)) {
                e.preventDefault();
                forceFocus = false;
                document.querySelector("#usntA40Link").focus();
            }
            if (!e.shiftKey && e.keyCode == 9 && e.target.isEqualNode(lastElm)) {
                e.preventDefault();
                forceFocus = false;
                document.querySelector("#aem-skip-links .skip-link").focus();
            }
        }
    }
    window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", (event) => {
        document.querySelector("#aem-skip-links .skip-link").addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
            let otBanner = document.querySelector("#onetrust-banner-sdk");
            if (e.keyCode === 9 && e.shiftKey && otBanner.offsetHeight > 0) {
                e.preventDefault();
                forceFocus = true;
                otBanner.focus();
            }
        });
        var skipLinks = document.querySelector("#aem-skip-links");
        if (skipLinks) {
            skipLinks.setAttribute('tabIndex', '-1');
            skipLinks.parentElement.prepend(skipLinks);
            var accessibilityLink = document.createElement("a");
            accessibilityLink.setAttribute("class", "accessibility-statement-link first-link");
            accessibilityLink.setAttribute("href", "https://www.usa.canon.com/website-accessibility");
            accessibilityLink.innerHTML = '<span>Accessibility Statement</span>';
            var accessbilityButton = document.querySelector("#usntA40Toggle");
            if (accessbilityButton) {
                accessbilityButton.querySelector("#usntA40Link").addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
                    if (!e.shiftKey && e.keyCode == 9) {
                        let otBanner = document.querySelector("#onetrust-banner-sdk");
                        if (otBanner && otBanner.offsetHeight > 0) {
                            forceFocus = true;
                            otBanner.focus();
                        }
                    }
                })
                accessbilityButton.parentElement.prepend(accessbilityButton);
            }
            skipLinks.parentElement.prepend(accessibilityLink);
            var linkClone = document.createElement("a");
            linkClone.setAttribute("class", "accessibility-statement-link first-link");
            linkClone.setAttribute("href", "#");
            linkClone.style = "opacity: 0";
            skipLinks.parentElement.prepend(linkClone);
            linkClone.focus();
            setTimeout(function () { linkClone.remove() }, 500);
        }
    });
    var addDomTimes = 1;
    const observerOT = new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
        mutations.forEach((mutation) => {
            mutation.addedNodes.forEach((node) => {
                if (node.id === 'onetrust-consent-sdk' && addDomTimes <= 2) {
                    if (!node.querySelector("#onetrust-banner-sdk")) {
                        return;
                    }
                    console.log("add dom");
                    document.querySelector("#onetrust-accept-btn-handler")?.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
                        setTimeout(function () {
                            var focusElm = document.querySelector("#aem-skip-links .skip-link");
                            var focusElmClone = focusElm.cloneNode(true);
                            focusElmClone.style = "opacity: 0";
                            focusElm.parentElement.append(focusElmClone);
                            focusElmClone.focus();
                            setTimeout(function () { focusElmClone.remove() }, 1000);
                        })
                    });
                    document.querySelector("#onetrust-reject-all-handler")?.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
                        setTimeout(function () {
                            var focusElm = document.querySelector("#aem-skip-links .skip-link");
                            var focusElmClone = focusElm.cloneNode(true);
                            focusElmClone.style = "opacity: 0";
                            focusElm.parentElement.append(focusElmClone);
                            focusElmClone.focus();
                            setTimeout(function () { focusElmClone.remove() }, 1000);
                        }, 100)
                    });
                    document.querySelector("#onetrust-banner-sdk")?.removeEventListener("keydown", handleEventFocusOfDialog);
                    document.querySelector("#onetrust-banner-sdk")?.addEventListener("keydown", handleEventFocusOfDialog);
                    setTimeout(function () {
                        var firstLink = document.querySelector(".accessibility-statement-link.first-link");
                        var accessibilityLinkClone = firstLink?.cloneNode(true);
console.log("accessibilityLinkClone",accessibilityLinkClone);
                        if (!accessibilityLinkClone) return;
                        accessibilityLinkClone.classList.remove("first-link");
                        accessibilityLinkClone.style = "opacity: 0;";
                        document.querySelector(".accessibility-statement-link")?.parentElement.prepend(accessibilityLinkClone);
                        accessibilityLinkClone.focus();
console.log("accessibilityLinkClone focus");
                        setTimeout(function () { accessibilityLinkClone.remove() }, 1e3);
                    }, 1500);
                    addDomTimes++
                }
            });
        });
    });
    observerOT.observe(document.body, {
        childList: true,
        subtree: true
    });
    document.addEventListener('readystatechange', function (e) {
        if (e.target.readyState === "complete") {
            $(document).ready(function () {
                var skipLinks = document.querySelector("#aem-skip-links");
                var accessbilityButton = document.querySelector("#usntA40Toggle");
                var accessibilityLink = document.querySelector(".accessibility-statement-link.first-link");
                var parentElm = skipLinks.parentElement;
                parentElm.prepend(skipLinks);
                parentElm.prepend(accessbilityButton);
                parentElm.prepend(accessibilityLink);
            });
        }
    });
</script>

<script>
    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        var footer = $('#footer-container');
            if (footer.length > 0) {
                function isVisible(element) {
                    var elementGetBound = element.getBoundingClientRect();
                    var top = elementGetBound.top;
                    var bottom = elementGetBound.bottom;
                    var vHeight = window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight;
                    return (top > 0 || bottom > 0) && top < vHeight;
                }
                function checkIE() {
                    return !!navigator.userAgent.match(/Trident/g) || !!navigator.userAgent.match(/MSIE/g);
                }
                function showImage(image) {
                    var imgsrc = $(image).attr('data-img');
                    var img = '';
                    if ($(image).hasClass('social_icons')) {
                        if (!$(image).hasClass('loaded')) {
                            var imgsrcHover = $(image).attr('data-imghover');
                            img = '<img width="280" height="360" class="custom-social-icon fluidimage-fit" src="' + domain.slice(0, -1) + imgsrc + '" alt="' + imgsrc + '-icon"><img class="custom-social-icon-hover fluidimage-fit" src="' + domain.slice(0, -1) + imgsrcHover + '"  alt="' + imgsrc + '-icon">';
                            $(image).append(img);
                            $(image).addClass('loaded');
                        }

                    }
                    else if ($(image).hasClass('browser-link')) {
                        if (!$(image).hasClass('loaded')) {
                            var imgTitle = $(image).attr('data-imgalt');
                            img = '<img width="280" height="360" class="browser-icon" src="' + domain.slice(0, -1) + imgsrc + '" alt="' + imgTitle + '">';
                            $(image).prepend(img);
                            $(image).addClass('loaded');
                        }
                    }
                }
                function initImg() {
                    if (checkIE() === false && footer.find(".best-browsers").length > 0) {
                        footer.find(".best-browsers").remove();
                    }
                    for (var i = 0; i < footer.find('a.icon-lazy').length; i++) {
                        var img = footer.find('a.icon-lazy')[i];
                        if (isVisible(img)) {
                            showImage(img);
                        }
                    }
                }
                initImg();
                window.addEventListener('scroll', initImg);
            }
    });
</script>
